### Task 16: Playwright end-to-end tests

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/e2e/surf.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `trackConsoleErrors` (foundation `tests/e2e/helpers.ts`); `window.__surf` (T14/T15); roles/test ids from T14 (`LEFT`, `RIGHT`, `DROP IN` buttons, `data-active`, `score`, `debug-panel`, `PAUSED`).
- Produces: the spec's browser checks — `/surf` boots, the menu is selectable, the run starts, simulated keys change the score, no console errors — plus pause, a swallowed wipeout → results → go again, the draw-call/triangle budget, and `?debug`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests** — `tests/e2e/surf.spec.ts`

```ts
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { trackConsoleErrors } from './helpers';

async function dropIn(page: Page) {
  await page.goto('/surf');
  const drop = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'DROP IN' });
  await expect(drop).toBeVisible();
  await drop.click();
  await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__surf?.phase === 'playing');
}

test.describe('surf game', () => {
  test('boots to the title menu and the break side is selectable', async ({ page }) => {
    const errors = trackConsoleErrors(page);
    await page.goto('/surf');
    const left = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'LEFT' });
    const right = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'RIGHT' });
    await expect(right).toHaveAttribute('data-active', 'true');
    await page.keyboard.press('ArrowLeft');
    await expect(left).toHaveAttribute('data-active', 'true');
    await right.click();
    await expect(right).toHaveAttribute('data-active', 'true');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => (window.__surf?.frames ?? 0) > 10);
    expect(errors()).toEqual([]);
  });

  test('the run starts and simulated keys change the score without console errors', async ({ page }) => {
    const errors = trackConsoleErrors(page);
    await dropIn(page);
    const f0 = await page.evaluate(() => window.__surf!.frames);
    await page.waitForFunction((f) => (window.__surf?.frames ?? 0) > f + 30, f0);
    // Each clean ollie banks 100 after the 1.5 s combo window.
    await expect
      .poll(
        async () => {
          await page.keyboard.press('Space');
          await page.waitForTimeout(2500);
          return page.evaluate(() => window.__surf?.score ?? 0);
        },
        { timeout: 60_000, intervals: [0] },
      )
      .toBeGreaterThan(0);
    await expect(page.getByTestId('score')).not.toHaveText('0');
    expect(errors()).toEqual([]);
  });

  test('Esc pauses and resumes', async ({ page }) => {
    await dropIn(page);
    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
    await expect(page.getByText('PAUSED')).toBeVisible();
    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__surf?.phase === 'playing');
  });

  test('holding stall gets you swallowed and shows the results screen', async ({ page }) => {
    await dropIn(page);
    await page.keyboard.down('ArrowDown');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__surf?.phase === 'results', undefined, { timeout: 60_000 });
    await page.keyboard.up('ArrowDown');
    await expect(page.getByText(/SWALLOWED BY THE BARREL/)).toBeVisible();
    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__surf?.phase === 'playing');
  });

  test('stays inside the draw-call and triangle budget', async ({ page }) => {
    await dropIn(page);
    await page.waitForTimeout(1500);
    const { calls, triangles } = await page.evaluate(() => window.__surf!);
    expect(calls).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(calls).toBeLessThan(80);
    expect(triangles).toBeLessThan(150_000);
  });

  test('?debug shows the live tuning panel', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/surf?debug');
    await expect(page.getByTestId('debug-panel')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText('physics.drive')).toBeVisible();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Measure on desktop (production build)**

Run: `npm run build && npm start`, open `http://localhost:3000/surf?debug` in Chrome on the M1 MacBook Air, full-screen, and ride for 60 s including a tube and a wipeout.
Expected (read from the debug panel): ≥ 58 fps sustained, calls < 80 (≈ 22–26: wave 2, sea 1, reef 2, islands 1, pier 1, gulls 1, sky 1, sun 1, flares 4, particles 1, board 2, surfer 1, +2 post passes, + gizmo when on), triangles < 150k (≈ 30k). If fps is below target, capture a Chrome performance profile and fix the hottest function first — likely suspects and fixes: `crestT` called too often per tick (cache the last `(x, result)` pair inside `WaveShape` keyed on `x` and `configVersion()`), or particle attribute uploads (limit `needsUpdate` ranges with `addUpdateRange` to the live span).

- [ ] **Step 3: Measure on a phone**

Run: `npm start -- -H 0.0.0.0`; on a recent mid-range phone on the same network open `http://<mac-ip>:3000/surf?debug` in landscape.
Expected: ≥ 30 fps, touch pad + OLLIE/grab buttons visible and responsive, RotateDevice overlay in portrait. Only if below 30 fps, lower the internal resolution on coarse pointers — change the renderer line from Step 1 to `this.retro = new RetroRenderer(canvas, coarse ? { internalHeight: 360 } : {});` — and re-measure.

- [ ] **Step 4: Playtest checklist (feel)** — play 10 runs on each side and tick each item; fix by adjusting the listed `SURF_CONFIG` knobs live in `?debug`, then copy the final values into `src/surf/config.ts` and re-run `npx vitest run src/surf/physics` (the headless invariants must still pass).

| Check | Target | Knobs |
|---|---|---|
| Drop in feels immediate | surfer is up and dropping, no dead frames | `Surfer.reset` defaults (x 4, t 0.55) |
| Riding straight | stays mid-face near the pocket, slowly drifts to the shoulder, never auto-wipes | `drive`, `drag` (ratio), `lift` |
| Shoulder pulls you back | riding out past ~30 m slows you noticeably | `drive`, `wave.shoulderLength` |
| Carve response | a full ← or → turns ~90° in ≈ 0.6 s at cruising speed | `carveRate`, `carveHalfSpeed` |
| Pumping | rhythmic ↑ every ~0.6 s clearly accelerates; mashing does not | `pumpImpulse`, `pumpCost` |
| Airs | a hard carve up the face from the pocket launches; ollie is small | `launchSpeed`, `ollieImpulse` |
| 360 is achievable | fast launches allow a 360 + landing | `spinRate` (spec 540), `launchSpeed` |
| Barrel | stall from the pocket gets tubed in ≈ 2 s; holding it swallows you in ≈ 2 s more | `stallDragMultiplier` (spec ×4), `drag` |
| Camera | never clips inside the wave on the chase; tube view shows the exit | `CAMERA_OFFSETS`, `camera.stiffness` |
| Audio | music ducks/muffles in the tube and recovers on exit; no clicks | `tubeCutoffHz`, `TAU` |

- [ ] **Step 5: Document** — append to `README.md`

```markdown

## Surf game (`/surf`)

| Input | On the face | In the air |
|---|---|---|
| ← / → | Carve (screen-relative) | Spin |
| ↑ | Pump (rhythm beats mashing) | — |
| ↓ | Stall (the curl catches you) | — |
| Space | Ollie | — |
| W / A / S / D (hold) | — | Method / Rail / Stalefish / Indy |
| Esc | Pause | Pause |

- Mobile (landscape): left thumb pad, right OLLIE + grab buttons.
- `/surf?debug` — live tuning sliders for `src/surf/config.ts`, fps / draw calls / triangles, wave-frame gizmo.
- Your avatar: edit `SURFER_LOOK` in `src/surf/config.ts` (hair, skin, outfit colors, board text, or `boardImage` for your own deck art ≤ 256 px).
- Soundtrack: tracks with `"surf": true` in `content/tracks/tracks.json`.
- High scores are stored locally (`localStorage['zf-surf-highscores']`).
```
